출처: [ROMS 빌드·전처리·옵션·변경 이력과 코드 대조](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/roms-build-options-history-vs-code.md) §2·§3·§5와 최종 전수 검증 기록.

## 문서와 코드 차이 (10-14 대조)

문서 판: ROMS 위키 wikitext, 2026-05-03 받음, 쪽별 revid는 [manifest.json](../raw/manuals/wiki/manifest.json). 코드 판: 원본 저장소 HEAD `57aecf58`, 2026-09-18, roms-4.2 이후 4.3 개발.

| build-options-history:RBH-0795 | EAST_TNUDGING 설명 | Options.wiki:1143 `:Option to use tracer passive/active nudging term for the eastern open boundary condition.` | 문서와 맞는 판: `roms-3.5 (bf4c05eb)`.<br>roms-3.5:ROMS/Nonlinear/t3dbc_im.F:231 `#   ifdef EAST_TNUDGING`<br>roms-3.5:ROMS/Nonlinear/t3dbc_im.F:232 `            tau=Tobc_out(itrc,ng,ieast)`<br>roms-3.5:ROMS/Nonlinear/t3dbc_im.F:233 `            IF ((dTdt*dTdx).lt.0.0_r8) tau=Tobc_in(itrc,ng,ieast)`<br>roms-3.5:ROMS/Nonlinear/t3dbc_im.F:234 `            tau=tau*dt(ng)`<br>roms-3.5:ROMS/Nonlinear/t3dbc_im.F:235 `#   endif`<br>roms-3.5:ROMS/Nonlinear/t3dbc_im.F:209 `#  if defined EAST_TRADIATION`<br>`e8719fb7 2011-09-23 src:ticket:519`<br>e8719fb7:ROMS/Nonlinear/t3dbc_im.F:230 `              IF (LBC(ieast,isTvar(itrc),ng)%nudging) THEN`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/t3dbc_im.F:253 `                IF (LBC(ieast,isTvar(itrc),ng)%nudging) THEN`<br>조회: `git log -S EAST_TNUDGING -- ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h`.<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/t3dbc_im.F:232 `        IF (LBC(ieast,isTvar(itrc),ng)%radiation) THEN`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/t3dbc_im.F:3 `#ifdef SOLVE3D`<br>ROMS/Utility/inp_decode.F:1802 `            CASE ('RADNUD')`<br>ROMS/Utility/inp_decode.F:1803 `              S(ib,ifield,igrid)%radiation = .TRUE.`<br>ROMS/Utility/inp_decode.F:1804 `              S(ib,ifield,igrid)%nudging = .TRUE.`<br>ROMS/Utility/inp_decode.F:1805 `              S(ib,ifield,igrid)%acquire = .TRUE.` | A 판 차이 | EAST_TNUDGING는 roms-3.5의 동쪽 추적자 경계 옵션이다. 현재 선택은 `LBC(ieast,isTvar(itrc),ng)%nudging`이다. nudging은 radiation 분기 안에서 적용한다. 이 경계 루틴은 SOLVE3D를 켠 빌드에 포함된다. git 이력 판정 근거: [ROMS-git-verify-A2.md](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/ROMS-git-verify-A2.md). Compute interior convective diffusivity due to static instability`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/lmd_vmix.F:522 `!  mixing.`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/lmd_vmix.F:524 `# ifdef LMD_CONVEC`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/lmd_vmix.F:525 `            cff=MAX(bvf(i,j,k),lmd_bvfcon)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/lmd_vmix.F:526 `            cff=MIN(1.0_r8,(lmd_bvfcon-cff)/lmd_bvfcon)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/lmd_vmix.F:527 `            nu_sxc=1.0_r8-cff*cff`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/lmd_vmix.F:528 `            nu_sxc=nu_sxc*nu_sxc*nu_sxc`<br>ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:276 `** LMD_CONVEC              to add convective mixing due to shear instability **`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/lmd_vmix.F:3 `#if defined NONLINEAR && defined LMD_MIXING && defined SOLVE3D`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/lmd_vmix.F:536 `            Akv(i,j,k)=Akv(i,j,k)+lmd_nu0c*nu_sxc`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/lmd_vmix.F:537 `            Akt(i,j,k,itemp)=Akt(i,j,k,itemp)+lmd_nu0c*nu_sxc`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/lmd_vmix.F:538 `# ifdef SALINITY`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/lmd_vmix.F:539 `            Akt(i,j,k,isalt)=Akt(i,j,k,isalt)+lmd_nu0c*nu_sxc` | B 문서 오기 | LMD_CONVEC는 정적 불안정에 의한 대류 혼합 옵션이다. NONLINEAR·LMD_MIXING·SOLVE3D 빌드에서 LMD_CONVEC를 켜면 bvf에 따른 대류 혼합 항을 Akv와 수온 Akt에 더한다. 염분 Akt에는 SALINITY를 켠 경우에 더한다. [10-14 결론 검증 보완] |

| build-options-history:RBH-1169 | NORTH_M3NUDGING 설명 | Options.wiki:2136 `:Option to use 3D momentum passive/active nudging term for the northern open boundary condition.` | roms-3.5 (`bf4c05eb`).<br>`roms-3.5:ROMS/Nonlinear/v3dbc_im.F:208` `#  if defined NORTH_M3RADIATION`<br>`roms-3.5:ROMS/Nonlinear/v3dbc_im.F:222` `#   ifdef NORTH_M3NUDGING`<br>`roms-3.5:ROMS/Nonlinear/v3dbc_im.F:223` `            IF ((dVdt*dVde).lt.0.0_r8) THEN`<br>`roms-3.5:ROMS/Nonlinear/v3dbc_im.F:224` `              tau=M3obc_in(ng,inorth)`<br>`roms-3.5:ROMS/Nonlinear/v3dbc_im.F:225` `            ELSE`<br>`roms-3.5:ROMS/Nonlinear/v3dbc_im.F:226` `              tau=M3obc_out(ng,inorth)`<br>`roms-3.5:ROMS/Nonlinear/v3dbc_im.F:227` `            END IF`<br>`e8719fb7 2011-09-23 src:ticket:519`<br>`e8719fb7:ROMS/Nonlinear/v3dbc_im.F:224` `              IF (LBC(inorth,isVvel,ng)%nudging) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/v3dbc_im.F:254` `                IF (LBC(inorth,isVvel,ng)%nudging) THEN`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/u3dbc_im.F:3 `#ifdef SOLVE3D`<br>ROMS/Utility/inp_decode.F:1802 `            CASE ('RADNUD')`<br>ROMS/Utility/inp_decode.F:1803 `              S(ib,ifield,igrid)%radiation = .TRUE.`<br>ROMS/Utility/inp_decode.F:1804 `              S(ib,ifield,igrid)%nudging = .TRUE.`<br>ROMS/Utility/inp_decode.F:1805 `              S(ib,ifield,igrid)%acquire = .TRUE.` | A 판 차이 | NORTH_M3NUDGING의 설명은 roms-3.5의 CPP 경계 선택과 일치한다. 현재 대응 선택은 `LBC(inorth,isUvel,ng)%nudging`와 `LBC(inorth,isVvel,ng)%nudging`이다. 과거와 현재 모두 radiation 분기 안에서 nudging을 적용한다. 이 경계 루틴은 SOLVE3D를 켠 빌드에 포함된다. git 이력 판정 근거: [ROMS-git-verify-A3.md](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/ROMS-git-verify-A3.md).
